# HD texture catalogue — sources and credits

Generated by `tools/textures/build_catalogue.py` from `tools/textures/sources/` and the verification records `tools/textures/verified.jsonl`. Catalogue revision `2026-10-20.809cfd2df68f`.

NeoStation hosts no texture. A pack marked *one tap* is downloaded from its author's own link when the user chooses it, checked against the size and SHA-256 measured here, and installed with the app's own installer into the engine's folder for the game. A *manual* pack is only a link to its author's page. Authors keep their rights; "no licence stated" grants nothing. Packs derived from commercial games are for use with the user's own copy.

Verification (`tools/textures/verify_packs.py`, see `docs/build486-hd-textures-catalogue.md`): the author's file was downloaded, recognised as a ZIP or 7z (never a web page), installed with the production code, and the installed folder checked as the engine reads it (Dolphin tex1_ names and DDS formats, PPSSPP textures.ini, Azahar pack.json options and PNG integrity, the Build 485 store for PS2). It does not show that an engine displays the textures in a game.

Manual PlayStation 2 entries noted "Listed in ps2ktxpak" are derived from the [ps2ktxpak catalogue](https://github.com/ps2ktxpak/catalog) (commit `807733e5`), ARMSX2's PlayStation 2 texture pack catalogue: game titles, serials, creators and the creators' own pages, completed with the PCSX2 GameIndex (`tools/textures/import_ps2ktxpak.py`). ps2ktxpak is MIT-licensed, Copyright (c) 2026 ps2ktxpak contributors; its notice is shipped with the catalogue (`packages/neo_texture_catalog/ios/Resources/ps2ktxpak-LICENSE.txt`). The packs themselves belong to their creators, under the terms shown on their pages.

A *manual, verified file* pack is a link to its author's page on a file host (MEGA, Google Drive, MediaFire, Dropbox, GameBanana): the app never downloads from these hosts, but the author's file was fetched once on CI (`tools/textures/hosts.py`) and installed with the production code, and the app installs that exact file (size and SHA-256) when the user imports it from the pack's page.

| Console | One tap | Manual, verified file | Manual | Games |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| PlayStation 2 (ARMSX2) | 45 | 1 | 652 | 763 |
| GameCube (Dolphin) | 30 | 71 | 8 | 65 |
| Wii (Dolphin) | 24 | 38 | 4 | 36 |
| PSP (PPSSPP) | 68 | 29 | 17 | 117 |
| Nintendo 3DS (Azahar) | 11 | 37 | 14 | 60 |
| Nintendo 64 (Mupen64Plus-Next, GLideN64) | 21 | 0 | 0 | 11 |
| PlayStation (Beetle PSX HW, Vulkan) | 2 | 0 | 0 | 4 |
| NES / Famicom (Mesen) | 5 | 0 | 0 | 5 |

A pack serving GameCube discs and Wii Virtual Console titles is counted for both consoles.

- MK64 Reloaded (DDS): README lists 19 contributors.
- MK64 Reloaded (PNG 4K): README lists 19 contributors.
- MK64 Reloaded (PNG HD): README lists 19 contributors.

- Super Mario Galaxy — GLXY repaint (Dolphin custom textures): README: some interface textures come from Sy24's faithful HD textures mod, edited for GLXY.

- Wip3out Special Edition Beta Texturepack: README: textures from the official Wipeout 3 US demo (Official U.S. PlayStation Magazine #25; Psygnosis Leeds, Sony, Studio Liverpool), found and ported by Matsilagi; Concept Assegai skin by Shara; Concept Icaras skin by Matsilagi; XentaX PBP/CMP scripts and TIMViewer used to read the textures.
